**parameters**　[{"parameter": "service_name", "label_zh": "知識圖譜服務名稱", "label_en": "Knowledge Graph Service Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the knowledge graph service."}, {"parameter": "summary", "label_zh": "摘要 （可選）", "label_en": "Summary (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The summary description of the knowledge graph."}, {"parameter": "tags", "label_zh": "標簽 （可選）", "label_en": "Tags (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The keywords or terms that describe the knowledge graph."}, {"parameter": "portal_folder", "label_zh": "門戶文件夾 （可選）", "label_en": "Portal Folder (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The portal folder that will contain the knowledge graph."}, {"parameter": "share_with_org", "label_zh": "與組織共享 （可選）", "label_en": "Share with Organization (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the knowledge graph will be shared with all authenticated users in your organization. Checked—The knowledge graph will be shared with all authenticated users in your organization. Unchecked—The knowledge graph will not be shared with all authenticated users in your organization. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "sharing_groups", "label_zh": "共享群組 （可選）", "label_en": "Sharing Groups (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the groups that you are a member of that the knowledge graph will be shared with. Group—The group with which the knowledge graph will be shared."}, {"parameter": "data_store", "label_zh": "資料存儲 （可選）", "label_en": "Data Store (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the data store where the knowledge graph entities and relationships will be stored. Hosted graph store—The knowledge graph entities and relationships will be created and stored in the ArcGIS Graph Data Store associated with the Enterprise portal."}, {"parameter": "data_managed", "label_zh": "管理資料類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Data Managed Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies how data in a NoSQL database will be managed. ArcGIS managed—ArcGIS Knowledge will create, manage, and delete the entities and relationships stored in the NoSQL database. The data model stored in the graph store will be updated automatically when entity types and relationship types and their properties change. This option is only available if the NoSQL database is empty. This is the default. User managed—The data and data model will be read-only when they are accessed by ArcGIS Knowledge. You must create, manage, and delete the entities and relationships and define the data model using their NoSQL database."}, {"parameter": "unique_id", "label_zh": "唯一 ID （可選）", "label_en": "Unique ID (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The property in the graph database that contains a unique string value for each entity and relationship."}, {"parameter": "enable_editing", "label_zh": "啟用編輯 （可選）", "label_en": "Enable editing (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether editing operations will be enabled for users with sufficient privileges. Checked—Editing operations will be enabled in the knowledge graph. Unchecked—Editing operations will not be enabled in the knowledge graph. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "enable_add", "label_zh": "啟用添加 （可選）", "label_en": "Enable add (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether editors can add entities and relationships to the knowledge graph and define new entity types and relationship types. Checked—Editors can add entities and relationships to the knowledge graph. They can define new entity types and relationship types, and add properties. This is the default when the Enable editing parameter is checked. Unchecked—Editors cannot add entities or relationships to the knowledge graph, define new entity and relationship types, or add properties."}, {"parameter": "enable_delete", "label_zh": "啟用刪除 （可選）", "label_en": "Enable delete (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether editors can delete features in the knowledge graph. Checked—Editors can delete features. This is the default when the Enable editing parameter is checked. Unchecked—Editors cannot delete features."}, {"parameter": "enable_update", "label_zh": "啟用更新 （可選）", "label_en": "Enable update (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether editors can update the properties of entities and relationships and the geometry of an entity. Checked—Editors can update the properties of entities and relationships and the geometry of an entity. This is the default when the Enable editing parameter is checked. Unchecked—Editors cannot update properties of entities and relationships or the geometry of an entity."}, {"parameter": "update_options", "label_zh": "更新選項 （可選）", "label_en": "Update Option (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the properties of entities and relationships and the geometry of an entity can be updated or only the properties of entities and relationships. Properties and geometry—The properties of entities and relationships and the geometry of entities can be updated. This is the default if editing has been enabled. Properties only—Only the properties of entities and relationships can be updated."}, {"parameter": "enable_search", "label_zh": "啟用搜索選項 （可選）", "label_en": "Enable search option (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether full-text search will be enabled for graph items using their properties. Checked—Full-text search will be enabled for graph items using their properties. This is the default for a hosted graph store or ArcGIS-managed NoSQL data store. Unchecked—Full-text search will not be enabled for graph items using their properties."}, {"parameter": "document_entity_name", "label_zh": "文檔實體名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Document Entity Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the entity type in the data model that represents documents that are referenced by the knowledge graph. The default is Document."}, {"parameter": "has_document_relationship_name", "label_zh": "文檔關系名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Document Relationship Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the relationship type in the data model that represents a relationship between an entity in the knowledge graph and a document associated with that entity. The default is HasDocument."}, {"parameter": "document_properties", "label_zh": "文檔屬性 （可選）", "label_en": "Document Properties (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Specifies the set of properties that will be created for each document entity. Value table columns: Text—The extracted text from a document. The default property name is text. URL—The location of the document. The default property name is url. Keywords—The keywords that will be used to search for the document. The default property name is keywords. Name—The file name of the document. The default property name is name. Content Type—The type of data contained by the referenced document. This property is stored as a Multipurpose Internet Mail Extensions (MIME) type. The default property name is contentType. Title—The title of the document. The default property name is title. Metadata—The metadata describing the document. The default property name is metadata. File Extension—The file extension when the referenced document is a file. The default property name is fileExtension. Geometry—The spatial property associated with a document. The default property name is geometry."}, {"parameter": "max_records", "label_zh": "服務器返回的最大記錄數 （可選）", "label_en": "Maximum Records Returned by Server (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The number of graph items that will be returned and displayed by an openCypher query. A large number may result in slower performance. The default value is 10,000."}, {"parameter": "max_records_search", "label_zh": "服務器在搜索操作期間返回的最大記錄數 （可選）", "label_en": "Maximum Records Returned by Server During Search (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The number of graph items that can be returned by a search using the indexed properties of entities and relationships. A large number may result in slower performance. The default value is 2,000."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.kg.CreateKnowledgeGraph(service_name, {summary}, {tags}, {portal_folder}, {share_with_org}, {sharing_groups}, {data_store}, {data_managed}, {unique_id}, {enable_editing}, {enable_add}, {enable_delete}, {enable_update}, {update_options}, {enable_search}, {document_entity_name}, {has_document_relationship_name}, {document_properties}, {max_records}, {max_records_search})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：service_name、summary、tags、portal_folder、share_with_org、sharing_groups、data_store、data_managed、unique_id、enable_editing、enable_add、enable_delete、enable_update、update_options、enable_search、document_entity_name、has_document_relationship_name、document_properties、max_records、max_records_search。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**chapter**　
- 工具參考

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03551-03575-003554.html#solution

## 001943: 輸入選擇和輸出網絡邏輯示意圖必須屬于同一邏輯示意圖資料集版本。 · 描述

**id**　9d91d04641dd927ac6-1

**english**　001943: Input selection and output network diagram must belong to the same database version.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1901-2000

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01926-01950-001943.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01926-01950-001943.html

**text**　為輸入網絡圖徵或網絡對象所指定的覆蓋或追加操作的資料庫版本與輸入邏輯示意圖版本不同。

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

**text**　CreateNetworkDatasetFromTemplate 示例 2（獨立腳本）
以下獨立 Python 腳本演示了如何使用 CreateNetworkDatasetFromTemplate 工具從現有模板創建新網絡資料集。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 通過模板創建網絡資料集 (Network Analyst 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　9da55c7fec85f3603c-14

**english**　Create Network Dataset From Template

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 網絡資料集工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/create-network-dataset-from-template.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/create-network-dataset-from-template.html

**text**　# Name: NetworkDatasetTemplate_workflow.py
# Description: Create a new network dataset with the same schema as an existing
#               network dataset
# Requirements: Network Analyst extension

#Import system modules
import arcpy
import os

try:
    #Set local variables
    original_network = "C:/data/Region1.gdb/Transportation/Streets_ND"
    new_network_location = "C:/data/Region2.gdb/Transportation"
    xml_template = "C:/data/NDTemplate.xml"

    #Create an XML template from the original network dataset
    arcpy.na.CreateTemplateFromNetworkDataset(original_network, xml_template)

    #Create the new network dataset in the output location using the template.
    #The output location must already contain feature classes and tables with
    #the same names and schema as the original network.
    arcpy.na.CreateNetworkDatasetFromTemplate(xml_template,
                                                new_network_location)

    #Build the new network dataset
    arcpy.na.BuildNetwork(os.path.join(new_network_location, "Streets_ND"))

except Exception as e:
    # If an error occurred, print line number and error message
    import traceback, sys
    tb = sys.exc_info()[2]
    print(("An error occurred on line %i" % tb.tb_lineno))
    print((str(e)))

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Create Network Dataset From Template，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。網絡資料集模板 [network_dataset_template]、輸出圖徵資料集 [output_feature_dataset]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

## 折線轉網格 (轉換工具) · 用法

**id**　9dc13909678bd428c9-2

**english**　Polyline to Raster

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 轉換工具箱
- 轉為網格工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/polyline-to-raster.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/polyline-to-raster.html

**text**　可以將包含折線圖徵的任意圖徵類（地理資料庫、shapefile 或 coverage）轉換為網格資料集。
輸入欄位類型決定輸出網格的類型。 如果欄位為整型，則輸出網格將為整型；如果為浮點型，則輸出將為浮點型。 如果輸入欄位包含字符串值，輸出網格將包含整數值欄位和字符串欄位。
像元大小可以通過數值進行定義，也可以從現有網格資料集獲取。 如果沒有將像元大小明確指定為參數值，則將從環境像元大小獲取相應值（前提是已指定環境）。 如果未指定參數像元大小或環境像元大小，但已設置了環境捕捉網格，則將使用捕捉網格的像元大小。 如果未指定任何內容，則像元大小會通過使用范圍的寬度或高度中的較小值除以 250 來計算，其中范圍位于在環境中指定的輸出座標系內。
如果使用數值指定了像元大小，工具將直接將其用于輸出網格。 如果使用網格資料集指定了像元大小，則參數將顯示網格資料集的路徑而不是像元大小值。 該網格資料集的像元大小將直接用于分析，前提是資料集的空間參考與輸出空間參考相同。 如果資料集的空間參考不同，則將基于所選的像元大小投影方法進行投影。
此工具與網格轉折線 工具互為補充，后者是將網格轉換為折線圖徵類。
當輸出像元中存在多個圖徵時，該工具比圖徵轉網格 工具更能控制像元值的分配。
對于支持空值的資料格式（例如文件地理資料庫圖徵類），當用作輸入時，空值將被忽略。
特定網格存儲環境 可能會應用于此工具。 對于金字塔 環境，僅支持構建金字塔設置。 將忽略其余金字塔環境設置。 在后續步驟中，可以使用構建金字塔 工具，進一步控制金字塔的性質。 對于壓縮 環境，僅支持壓縮類型。 這適用于除 Esri Grid 以外的網格格式。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/conversion/polyline-to-raster.html#usage

- 說明

**values**　
- cell_assignment （可選）
- 此方法用于在多個圖徵位于一個像元中時，確定為該像元分配值的方式。 MAXIMUM_LENGTH—像元內具有最大長度的圖徵可確定將分配給像元的值。 這是默認設置。 MAXIMUM_COMBINED_LENGTH—如果像元中有多個圖徵具有相同的值，則合并這些圖徵的長度。 像元內具有最大長度的組合圖徵可確定將分配給像元的值。
- String

## 折線轉網格 (轉換工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　9dc13909678bd428c9-22

**english**　Polyline to Raster

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 轉換工具箱
- 轉為網格工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/polyline-to-raster.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/polyline-to-raster.html

**text**　# Name: PolylineToRaster_Ex_02.py
# Description: Converts polyline features to a raster dataset.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data"

# Set local variables
inFeatures = "roads.shp"
valField = "CLASS"
outRaster = "c:/output/roads.tif"
assignmentType = "MAXIMUM_COMBINED_LENGTH"
priorityField = "LENGTH"
cellSize = 30

# Run PolylineToRaster
arcpy.conversion.PolylineToRaster(inFeatures, valField, outRaster,
                                  assignmentType, priorityField, cellSize)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/conversion/polyline-to-raster.html#parameters

## 折線轉網格 (轉換工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 轉換工具箱
- 轉為網格工具集

**english**　Polyline to Raster

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/polyline-to-raster.html

**id**　9dc13909678bd428c9-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/polyline-to-raster.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Polyline to Raster，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖徵 [in_features]、值欄位 [value_field]、輸出網格資料集 [out_rasterdataset]、像元分配類型 （可選） [cell_assignment]、優先級欄位 （可選） [priority_field]、像元大小 （可選） [cellsize]、構建網格屬性表 （可選） [build_rat]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/conversion/polyline-to-raster.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_features", "label_zh": "輸入圖徵", "label_en": "Input Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The polyline input feature dataset to be converted to a raster."}, {"parameter": "value_field", "label_zh": "值欄位", "label_en": "Value field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field used to assign values to the output raster. It can be any field of the input feature dataset's attribute table."}, {"parameter": "out_rasterdataset", "label_zh": "輸出網格資料集", "label_en": "Output Raster Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The output raster dataset to be created. If the output raster will not be saved to a geodatabase, specify .tif for TIFF file format, .CRF for CRF file format, .img for ERDAS IMAGINE file format, or no extension for Esri Grid raster format."}, {"parameter": "cell_assignment", "label_zh": "像元分配類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Cell assignment type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The method to determine how the cell will be assigned a value when more than one feature falls within a cell. Maximum length—The feature with the longest length that covers the cell will determine the value to assign to the cell. This is the default. Maximum combined length—If there is more than one feature in a cell with the same value, the lengths of these features will be combined. The combined feature with the longest length within the cell will determine the value to assign to the cell."}, {"parameter": "priority_field", "label_zh": "優先級欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Priority field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "This field is used to determine which feature should take preference over another feature that falls over a cell. When it is used, the feature with the largest positive priority is always selected for conversion irrespective of the Cell assignment type chosen."}, {"parameter": "cellsize", "label_zh": "像元大小 （可選）", "label_en": "Cellsize (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Analysis Cell Size", "description_en": "The cell size of the output raster being created. This parameter can be defined by a numeric value or obtained from an existing raster dataset. If the cell size hasn't been explicitly specified as the parameter value, the environment cell size value is used, if specified; otherwise, additional rules are used to calculate it from the other inputs. See Usages for more detail."}, {"parameter": "build_rat", "label_zh": "構建網格屬性表 （可選）", "label_en": "Build raster attribute table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the output raster will have a raster attribute table. This parameter only applies to integer rasters. Checked—The output raster will have a raster attribute table. This is the default. Unchecked—The output raster will not have a raster attribute table."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.conversion.PolylineToRaster(in_features, value_field, out_rasterdataset, {cell_assignment}, {priority_field}, {cellsize}, {build_rat})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_features、value_field、out_rasterdataset、cell_assignment、priority_field、cellsize、build_rat。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160076-160100-160085.html#solution

## 180676: 輸入 Floorplan 圖層無效。 · 描述

**id**　9dc509f9aac150119c-1

**english**　180676: Input Floorplan layer is not valid.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 180601-180700

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/180001-190000/tool-errors-and-warnings-180676-180700-180676.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/180001-190000/tool-errors-and-warnings-180676-180700-180676.html

**text**　輸入樓層平面圖覆蓋區圖層 、 輸入樓層平面圖面圖層 和 輸入樓層平面圖折線圖層 參數值必須為使用 提取 BIM 文件樓層平面圖 工具創建的圖徵類或圖徵圖層，該工具作用的 IFC 文件與用于 輸入 BIM 文件工作空間 參數的文件相同。
如果已選中 從樓層平面圖圖層加載 參數，則您必須為所有輸入樓層平面圖圖層參數提供輸入樓層平面圖圖層。

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 002761: 版本受到保護。 如果您不是版本的所有者，則無法向受保護版本寫入內容。 · 描述

**id**　9dc94bfb9459a2aba4-1

**english**　002761: Version is protected. You can't write to a protected version if you are not the owner of the version.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告

## 設置流向 (追蹤網絡工具) · 用法

**id**　9df47a610fedafb292-2

**english**　Set Flow Direction

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 追蹤網絡工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/set-flow-direction.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/set-flow-direction.html

**text**　只能通過設置流向工具為參與版本 1 追蹤網絡的線圖徵設置流向。 舊版本： 追蹤網絡版本 2 或更高版本不支持設置流向工具。 使用 FLOWDIRECTION 網絡屬性欄位在網絡中設置或修改線圖徵的流向。
必須啟用 網絡拓撲。
可在圖徵級別將流向指定為沿著數字化方向、沿著與數字化方向相反的方向或不確定方向。 當地圖上存在選擇圖徵時，將僅為那些圖徵設置流向。 了解有關如何設置流向的詳細信息

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 設置流向 (追蹤網絡工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 追蹤網絡工具箱

**english**　Set Flow Direction

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/set-flow-direction.html

**id**　9df47a610fedafb292-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/set-flow-direction.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Set Flow Direction，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入追蹤網絡 [input_trace_network]、圖徵圖層 [in_edges]、流向 [flow_direction]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/trace-network/set-flow-direction.html#parameters

Note: This parameter requires a Trace Network Version 1 as input."}, {"parameter": "in_edges", "label_zh": "圖徵圖層", "label_en": "Feature Layers", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The polyline features that participate in the input trace network."}, {"parameter": "flow_direction", "label_zh": "流向", "label_en": "Flow Direction", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the flow direction of the edges. ## Import Digital Obstacle File (Aviation Tools) · 匯總

**id**　9e064e25e50a4d5f95-1

**english**　Import Digital Obstacle File

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料交換工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/import-digital-obstacle-file.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/import-digital-obstacle-file.html

**text**　Adds, deletes, and updates the obstacle point features in an input obstacle feature class using an input digital obstacle file (DOF).
A DOF provided by the FAA contains obstruction data for manufactured objects that affect domestic aeronautical charting or airport products. A DOF contains digital obstacle data within all FAA regions in addition to some areas of Canada, Mexico, the Caribbean, the Bahamas, and the Pacific. A DOF does not purport to indicate the presence of all obstructions that may be encountered during flight.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/import-digital-obstacle-file.html#summary

## Import Digital Obstacle File (Aviation Tools) · 用法

**id**　9e064e25e50a4d5f95-2

**english**　Import Digital Obstacle File

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料交換工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/import-digital-obstacle-file.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/import-digital-obstacle-file.html

**text**　The Input Digital Obstacle File parameter must reference a DOF with the .DAT file extension.
The Target Obstacle Features parameter value must be a point feature class containing obstacle features from an airport or charting geodatabase. For example, the Esri Airports schema provided in the ArcGIS Aviation Airports product files contains the Obstacles feature class that can be updated using this tool.
The tool will include a message detailing the number of features that were added, modified, or deleted.
The tool can be customized using a JSON configuration file in the ArcGIS Pro installation location Resources\Aviation directory. The DOFConfig.json file allows you to change how this tool processes a DOF. A custom configuration file allows for future changes to the FAA DOF specification or to the obstacle feature class schemas. In the absence of a configuration file, this tool will use its default configuration information. 注: When processing a DOF, the above mean sea level height value is written to the elevation field (Elev_Val or Elev). The above ground level height value is written to the height field (Height_Val or Height). The ASML elevation of the base of an obstacle can be determined by subtracting the Height_Val field value from the Elev_Val field value after import.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/import-digital-obstacle-file.html#usage

**id**　9e064e25e50a4d5f95-13

**english**　Import Digital Obstacle File

**chapter**

- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集

**text**　# Name: ImportDOF_example.py
# Description: Imports Digital Obstacle File (DOF) data into a feature class

import arcpy

# Set variables
dofFile = r'c:\data\DOF.dat'
features = r'c:\data\AIS.gdb\Obstacle'

# Run ImportDOF
arcpy.aviation.ImportDOF(dofFile, features)

## Import Digital Obstacle File (Aviation Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料交換工具集

**english**　Import Digital Obstacle File

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/import-digital-obstacle-file.html

**id**　9e064e25e50a4d5f95-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/import-digital-obstacle-file.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Import Digital Obstacle File，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Digital Obstacle File [in_obstacle_file]、Target Obstacle Features [obstacle_features]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/import-digital-obstacle-file.html#parameters

The contents of the DOF will be used to update the Target Obstacle Features parameter values."}, {"parameter": "obstacle_features", "label_zh": "Target Obstacle Features", "label_en": "Target Obstacle Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The point feature class that will contain obstacle information from the DOF after the tool is run."}]

## 110610: 發現鄰域具有超過 1000 個圖徵，這可能會對性能產生負面影響。 考慮使用更少的鄰域。 · 解決方案

**id**　9e09160d956c6424c0-2

**english**　110610: Neighborhood with more than 1000 features found which can negatively impact performance. Consider using fewer neighbors.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 110601-110700

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110601-110625-110610.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110601-110625-110610.html

**text**　不需要操作。 但是，建議您指定一個導致鄰域總數較少的鄰域配置。 如果您正在使用距離帶，請考慮減小其大小。 如果您正在使用面圖徵，請考慮使用鄰接代替鄰居數或距離帶。 或者，如果您正在使用點資料，請考慮使用 Delaunay 三角剖分或鄰居數。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110601-110625-110610.html#solution

如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。

工具的名稱

相關資料集詳細信息

我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 110538: 由于記錄超過 10,000 條，無法創建“按聚類時間跨度”圖表。 · 解決方案

**id**　9e4a74be9f4578044f-2

**english**　110538: The Time Span Per Cluster chart was not created because there are more than 10,000 records.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告

**kind**　說明

**text**　這是一條信息性消息。 不需要操作。
要查看記錄數小于或等于 10,000 的特定聚類的折線圖，選擇聚類的所有記錄并將其復制到新資料集。 可使用輸入日期欄位和聚類 ID 值創建 折線圖 。

## 點云工具集概覽 · 點云工具集概覽

**id**　9e52a1ca53b332fd0b-1

**english**　An overview of the Point Cloud toolset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/an-overview-of-the-point-cloud-toolset.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/an-overview-of-the-point-cloud-toolset.html

**text**　該點云工具集包含用于分類、管理和處理 LAS 格式點云資料的工具。 Esri 的專有地面和建筑物分類算法提供高質量結果，從而支持數字高程模型（DEM）生成和 3D 圖徵提取工作流程。 其他分類方法支持：
基于在地面或定制高程表面上方的高度對點分類。
使用點與點圖徵、線圖徵或面圖徵的鄰近程度對點分類。
使用分類網格，基于點疊加對點分類。
該工具集還包含深度學習工具，用以對點云中的目標分類并刪除點云中的目標。 可以創建并部署為機載和地面雷達和攝影測量點云量身打造的定制解決方案。
此外，該工具集提供了用于執行以下資料管理操作的功能：
將較大的 LAS 文件劃分為更高效的切片。
對點云進行稀疏化處理，以消除高密度點集合的熱點，這些熱點可能會降低性能并增加資料體積。
使用重疊影像為機載點云著色。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/an-overview-of-the-point-cloud-toolset.html
